Use this generated page to find the one page that owns each glossary concept, and the provisions that page holds. A concept is stated on its home page; every other page cites the concept rather than restating its rule.

## Notes

The `litespec-maintain provisions` command writes this page from the Home line of each glossary entry, and `litespec-maintain provisions --check` fails when it differs. Edit the glossary, not the page.
